id="infoSection"><h2>Status:</h2><p class="intro"> This is the original version (as it was originally made). This item of legislation is currently only available in its original format.</p></div><div id="viewLegContents" xml:lang="en"><div class="LegSnippet hasBlockAmends" id="viewLegSnippet"><div class="LegClearFix LegPrelims"><p class="LegBanner">Statutory Instruments</p><h1 class="LegNo">2026 No. 983</h1><p class="LegSubject">SANCTIONS</p><h1 class="LegTitle">The Iran (Sanctions) (Amendment) Regulations 2026</h1><div class="LegDate"><p class="LegDateText">Made</p><p class="LegDateDate">7th September 2026</p></div><div class="LegDate"><p class="LegDateText">Laid before Parliament</p><p class="LegDateDate">8th September 2026</p></div><div class="LegDate"><p class="LegDateText">Coming into force</p><p class="LegDateDate">29th September 2026</p></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="Legislation-Preamble"></a><div class="LegEnactingText"><p class="LegText">The Secretary of State(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00001" title="Go to footnote 1" id="Backf00001">1</a>), considering that the condition in section 45(2) of the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act 2018 (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-the-act">the Act</span>”)(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00002" title="Go to footnote 2" id="Backf00002">2</a>) is met, makes these Regulations in exercise of the powers conferred by sections 1, 3(1)(b), (c), (d), (e), (g) and (2)(c), 5, 6(1)(a) and (b), (2), (3), (6) and (7), 7(1) to (5), 10(2), 14, 15, 16(1), 17(2), (4), (5) and (6), 19(1) and (2), 20(1), 21(1), 45(1) and 54(1)(c) and (2)(a) of, and paragraphs 2, 3(a), (b), 4(a), (b) and (c), 5(a) (b) and (d), 6(a) and (b), 7(b), 11(a), 13(b), (c), (g), (h), (i), (k), (l), (m), (n), (q) and (w), 14(a), (f), (j), (k), 17, 19, 20(a), (b), (d) and (e) and 23 of Schedule 1 to, the Act.&#xD;</p></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-1"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPartFirst"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 1</span><span class="LegPartTitle">General</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Citation, commencement and extent</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-1">1.</span>—(1) These Regulations may be cited as the Iran (Sanctions) (Amendment) Regulations 2026.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) These Regulations come into force on 29th September 2026.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) These Regulations extend to England and Wales, Scotland and Northern Ireland.&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Interpretation</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-2">2.</span>  In these Regulations—&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00213"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-the-2019-regulations">the 2019 Regulations</span>” mean the Iran (Sanctions) (Nuclear) (EU Exit) Regulations 2019(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00003" title="Go to footnote 3" id="Backf00003">3</a>);&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="p00219"></a><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3">“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-the-2023-regulations">the 2023 Regulations</span>” mean the Iran (Sanctions) Regulations 2023(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00004" title="Go to footnote 4" id="Backf00004">4</a>).&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-2"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 2</span><span class="LegPartTitle">Amendment of the 2019 Regulations</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Amendment of the 2019 Regulations</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-3">3.</span>  The 2019 Regulations are amended in accordance with this Part.&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Interpretation and extra-territorial application</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-4">4.</span>—(1) In regulation 2 (interpretation)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-4-1-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">renumber the existing text as paragraph (1);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-4-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in paragraph (1), as so renumbered—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-4-1-b-i">(i)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">after the definition of “the Act”, insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">aircraft licence</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a licence issued by the Secretary of State under regulation 41A (aircraft licences);<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-4-1-b-ii">(ii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">after the definition of “document”, insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">the Dual-Use Regulation</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means Council Regulation </span><a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/european/regulation/2009/0428"><span class="LegAmendingText">(EC) No 428/2009</span></a><span class="LegAmendingText"> of 5 May 2009 setting up a Community regime for the control of exports, transfer, brokering and transit of dual-use items</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00005" title="Go to footnote 5" id="Backf00005">5</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">;<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-4-1-b-iia">(iia)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">after the definition of “the EU Iran Regulation”, insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">the Government of Iran</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” includes its public bodies, corporations or agencies, its armed forces or any person acting on its behalf or at its direction;<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-4-1-b-iii">(iii)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">after the definition of “resolution 2231”, insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">ships licence</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a licence issued by the Secretary of State under regulation 41B (ships licences);<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4No" id="regulation-4-1-b-iv">(iv)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4Text">after the definition of “trade licence”, insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">United Kingdom person</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in section 21 of the Act;<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-4-1-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after paragraph (1), insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In these Regulations, a person is to be regarded as connected with Iran if the person is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">an individual who is, or an association or combination of individuals who are, ordinarily resident in Iran,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">an individual who is, or an association or combination of individuals who are, located in Iran,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a person, other than an individual, which is incorporated or constituted under the law of Iran,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a person, other than an individual, which is domiciled in Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a person, other than an individual, which is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of regulation 7) by a person within any of sub-paragraphs (a) to (d).<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) After regulation 2, insert—&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Interpretation: commodity codes</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">2A.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of these Regulations—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a reference to a commodity code is a reference to the relevant corresponding provision in the Goods Classification Table;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a thing “falls within” a commodity code if it is, or would be, classified under that commodity code, as set out in the Goods Classification Table;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a thing “falls within” a chapter if it is, or would be, classified under that chapter, as set out in the Goods Classification Table;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">where a commodity code or chapter is preceded by “ex”, the goods specified in these Regulations constitute only a part of the scope of the commodity code or chapter and must fall within both the description given to that code or chapter in these Regulations and the scope of the code or chapter in the Goods Classification Table.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of determining whether or not a thing is, or would be, “classified” in accordance with paragraph (1), the rules of interpretation contained in the following have effect—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Part Two (Goods Classification Table Rules of Interpretation) of the Tariff of the United Kingdom;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">notes to a section or chapter of the Goods Classification Table.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">commodity code</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” includes a code denoting a heading or sub-heading;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">the Goods Classification Table</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means the table so named in Annex I in Part Three of the Tariff of the United Kingdom;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">the Tariff of the United Kingdom</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means the document containing the legal classification and import rate for products being imported into the United Kingdom, entitled “The Tariff of the United Kingdom”, as revised or re-issued from time to time, including by any document published under regulations made under section 8(1) of the Taxation (Cross-border Trade) Act 2018</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00006" title="Go to footnote 6" id="Backf00006">6</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText"> replacing the same in whole or in part.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In regulation 3 (application of prohibitions and requirements outside the United Kingdom)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-4-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after regulation 17, insert—</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Investments in relation to Iran</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">17A.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) must not directly or indirectly grant a loan or credit to a relevant person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”), where P knows or has reasonable cause to suspect that Q is a relevant person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) must not directly or indirectly acquire or extend a participation, or acquire any ownership interest, in a relevant person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) who is not an individual, where P knows or has reasonable cause to suspect that Q is a relevant person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) must not directly or indirectly create a joint venture with a relevant person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”), where P knows or has reasonable cause to suspect that Q is a relevant person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1), (2) and (3) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1), (2) or (3) commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation, a “relevant person” is any person connected with Iran engaged in—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">any thing falling within a commodity code mentioned in column 1 of the table in Part 2 of Schedule 4 to the 2023 Regulations, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">any item specified by description in Part 3 of Schedule 4 to the 2023 Regulations,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the exploration for, or production of, oil or gas,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the refining of fuels or the liquefaction of gas,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the petrochemical industry, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">uranium mining or the enrichment or reprocessing of uranium.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(7)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">the 2023 Regulations</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” mean the Iran (Sanctions) Regulations 2023;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">exploration</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”, in relation to oil and gas, includes the exploration for oil and gas reserves, prospecting for and management of oil and gas reserves, as well as the provision of geological services in relation to such reserves;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">petrochemical industry</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means production plants for the manufacturing of any of the items listed in Annex V to the EU Iran Regulation;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">production</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”, in relation to oil and gas, includes bulk gas transmission services for the purpose of transit or delivery to directly interconnected grids;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">refining</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means the processing, conditioning or preparation for the final sale of fuels.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">UK credit or financial institutions: accounts and corresponding banking relationships etc.</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">17B.</span></span>—<span or on behalf of such a person;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">authorise the taking up and pursuit of new business by a representative office, branch or subsidiary of a person falling within paragraph (5), if the representative office, branch or subsidiary was not already authorised for such a purpose before 8th September 2026.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person falling within paragraph (5) must not directly or indirectly acquire or extend a participation, or acquire any ownership interest, in a UK credit or financial institution.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The following persons fall within this paragraph—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS of business which forms a legally dependent part of that institution and which carries out all or some of the transactions inherent in the business of that institution;</span>&#xD;</p><p Iran</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p customers;</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div></div></div></div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span means—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span which by way of business—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegRomanList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel5NoAmend LegListItemNo">(i)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">operates a currency exchange office,</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel5NoAmend LegListItemNo">(ii)</div><p class="LegLevel5NoAmend LegListItemNo">(iii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">cashes cheques that are made payable to customers.</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div></div></div></div><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Insurance and reinsurance services</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">17C.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) must not provide insurance or reinsurance services to a person falling within paragraph (2), if P knows or has reasonable cause to suspect that P is providing such services to a person falling with paragraph (2).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) falls within this paragraph if—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q is connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q is acting on behalf of or at the direction of a person connected with Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes the prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Nothing in this regulation prohibits compliance with an insurance or reinsurance agreement concluded before 8th September 2026, provided that the agreement concerned was not prohibited at the date on which it was made.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">insurance and reinsurance services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” include the provision of services relating to the extension or renewal of an insurance or reinsurance agreement, except where there is an obligation, which arose before 8th September 2026, of the insurer or re-insurer to accept the extension or renewal;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">person acting at the direction of a person connected with Iran</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” does not include any person who is acting under a direction which is given in relation to a ship or aircraft for the purposes of docking, loading or unloading the ship or aircraft or for any purpose in connection with the safe transit of the ship or aircraft in the territorial sea adjacent to Iran or in the airspace above it.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Sale or purchase of bonds</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">17D.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) must not directly or indirectly—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">sell to a person falling within paragraph (4), or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">purchase from such a person,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Iranian bonds or bonds guaranteed by the Government of Iran and issued after 8th September 2026 if P knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that the sale or purchase of such bonds is directly or indirectly to or from such a person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) must not provide brokering services to a person falling within paragraph (4) in respect of Iranian bonds or bonds guaranteed by the Government of Iran and issued after 8th September 2026, if P knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that the person falls within paragraph (4).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) must not provide—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">brokering services,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">advertising services, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">any other service,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">if P knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that the provision of the service assists a person falling within paragraph (4) to issue Iranian bonds or bonds guaranteed by the Government of Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The following persons fall within this paragraph—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the Government of Iran,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a person falling within regulation 17B(5) (Iranian credit or financial institutions);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a person acting on behalf of or at the direction of a person falling within sub-paragraph (a), or (b);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a person, other than an individual, which is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of regulation 7) by a person falling within sub-paragraph (a), (b) or (c).</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) to (3) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1), (2) or (3) commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(7)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation, “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Iranian bonds</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means bonds issued by the Government of Iran.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">before regulation 18, insert as a Chapter heading—</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearFragmentNumber"></div><h3 class="LegChapterNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span LegP5No" id="regulation-7-b-ii-aa">(aa)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP5Text">before paragraph (a), insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(za)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">maritime technology,<span
