22, omit paragraph (4);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in regulation 27(4), omit the definitions of “brokering service”, and “third country”;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-e">(e)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after regulation 27, insert—</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearChapter"></div><h4 class="LegChapterFirst"><span class="LegChapterNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>Chapter 1A</span></span><span class="LegChapterTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Exports and related activities: gold, precious metals and diamonds</span></span></h4><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Export of gold, precious metals and diamonds to Iran</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27A.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The export of gold, precious metals or diamonds to, or for use in, Iran is prohibited.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Supply and delivery of gold, precious metals and diamonds to Iran</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27B.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly supply or deliver gold, precious metals or diamonds from a third country to a place in Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes the prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with that offence (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the gold, precious metals or diamonds were destined (or ultimately destined) for Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Making available gold, precious metals and diamonds</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27C.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly make gold, precious metals or diamonds available—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(a) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the gold, precious metals or diamonds were for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Technical assistance relating to gold, precious metals and diamonds</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27D.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide technical assistance relating to gold, precious metals or diamonds—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(a) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the gold, precious metals or diamonds were for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Financial services and funds relating to gold, precious metals and diamonds</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27E.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide, to a person connected with Iran, financial services in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement whose object or effect is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the export of gold, precious metals or diamonds,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect supply or delivery of gold, precious metals or diamonds,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">directly or indirectly making available gold, precious metals or diamonds to a person, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision of technical assistance relating to gold, precious metals or diamonds.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly make funds available to a person connected with Iran in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement mentioned in paragraph (1).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide financial services or funds in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement whose object or effect is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the export of gold, precious metals or diamonds to, or for use in, Iran,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect supply or delivery of gold, precious metals or diamonds to a place in Iran,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">directly or indirectly making available gold, precious metals or diamonds—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision of technical assistance relating to gold, precious metals or diamonds—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1), (2) and (3) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1), (2) or (3) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1) or (2) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (3) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the financial services or funds (as the case may be) were provided in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement mentioned in that paragraph.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Brokering services relating to gold, precious metals and diamonds</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27F.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide brokering services in relation to any arrangements described in regulation 27E(1) to (3).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes the prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">It is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the brokering services were provided in relation to an arrangement mentioned in that paragraph.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearChapter"></div><h4 class="LegChapter"><span class="LegChapterNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Chapter 1B</span></span><span class="LegChapterTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Exports and related activities: energy-related goods and energy-related technology</span></span></h4><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Interpretation</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27G.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this Chapter “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Iran</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” includes Iran's exclusive economic zone and continental shelf (which terms are to be interpreted in accordance with the United Nations Convention on the Law of the Sea)</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00007" title="Go to footnote 7" id="Backf00007">7</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Export of energy-related goods</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27H.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The export of energy-related goods to, or for use in, Iran is prohibited.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Supply and delivery of energy-related goods</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27I.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly supply or deliver energy-related goods from a third country to a place in Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the goods were destined (or ultimately destined) for Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Making energy-related goods and energy-related technology available</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27J.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly make energy-related goods or energy-related technology available—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1)(a) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with that offence (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1)(b) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with that offence (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the goods or technology were for use in Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Transfer of energy-related technology</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27K.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not transfer energy-related technology—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a place in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(a) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the transfer was to a place in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Technical assistance relating to energy-related goods and energy-related technology</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27L.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide technical assistance relating to energy-related goods or energy-related technology—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exception and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1)(a) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the goods or technology were for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Financial services and funds relating to energy-related goods and energy-related technology</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27M.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide, to a person connected with Iran, financial services in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement whose object or effect is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the export of energy-related goods,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect supply or delivery of energy-related goods,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">directly or indirectly making energy-related goods or energy-related technology available to a person,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the transfer of energy-related technology, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision of technical assistance relating to energy-related goods or energy-related technology.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly make funds available to a person connected with Iran in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement mentioned in paragraph (1).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide financial services or funds in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement whose object or effect is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the export of energy-related goods to, or for use in, Iran,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect supply or delivery of energy-related goods to a place in Iran,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">directly or indirectly making energy-related goods or energy-related technology available—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the transfer of energy-related technology—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a place in Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision of technical assistance relating to energy-related goods or energy-related technology—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) to (3) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in any of paragraphs (1) to (3) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening a prohibition in paragraph (1) or (2) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening a prohibition in paragraph (3) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the financial services or funds (as the case may be) were provided in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement mentioned that paragraph.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Brokering services: non-UK activity relating to energy-related goods and energy-related technology.</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27N.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) must not directly or indirectly provide brokering services in relation to an arrangement (“arrangement A”) whose object or effect is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect supply or delivery of energy-related goods from a third country to a place in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">directly or indirectly making energy-related goods or energy-related technology available in a third country for direct or indirect supply or delivery—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a place in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision, in a non-UK country, of technical assistance relating to energy-related goods or technology—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the transfer of energy-related technology from a place in a third country—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a place in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision, in a non-UK country, of financial services—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, where arrangement A, or any other arrangement in connection with which arrangement A is entered into, is an arrangement mentioned in regulation 27M(1), or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">where arrangement A, or any other arrangement in connection with which arrangement A is entered into, is an arrangement mentioned in regulation 27M(3);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(f)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">directly or indirectly making funds available, in a non-UK country, to a person connected with Iran, where arrangement A, or any other arrangement in connection with which arrangement A is entered into, is an arrangement mentioned in regulation 27M(1); or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(g)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision of funds from a non-UK country, where arrangement A, or any other arrangement in connection with which arrangement A is entered into, is an arrangement mentioned in regulation 27M(3).</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with that offence (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the brokering services were provided in relation to an arrangement mentioned in that paragraph.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation, “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">non-UK country</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a country that is not the United Kingdom.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Prohibition on providing relevant energy-related services</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27O.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not provide, directly or indirectly, relevant energy services.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes the prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with that offence (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that P was providing relevant energy services.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">oil or gas exploration</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” includes the exploration for and management of oil and gas reserves, as well as the provision of geological services in relation to such reserves;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">oil or gas production</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” includes bulk transmission services for the purpose of transit or delivery to directly interconnected grids;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">relevant energy services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any of the following services which are necessary for an oil or gas exploration project in Iran or an oil or gas production project in Iran—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">drilling;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">well testing;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(c)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">logging and completion services;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(d)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">supply of specialised floating vessels.</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div><div class="LegClearChapter"></div><h4 class="LegChapter"><span class="LegChapterNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Chapter 1C</span></span><span class="LegChapterTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Sectoral software goods and sectoral software and technology</span></span></h4><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Export of sectoral software goods</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27P.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The export of sectoral software goods to, or for use in, Iran is prohibited.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Supply and delivery of sectoral software goods</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27Q.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly supply or deliver sectoral software goods from a third country to a place in Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the goods were destined (or ultimately destined) for Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Making sectoral software goods and sectoral software and technology available</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27R.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly make sectoral software goods or sectoral software and technology available—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1)(a) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the sectoral software goods or sectoral software and technology were for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Transfer of sectoral software and technology</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27S.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not transfer sectoral software and technology—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a place in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(a) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the transfer was to a place in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Technical assistance relating to sectoral software goods, and sectoral software and technology</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27T.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide technical assistance relating to sectoral software goods or sectoral software and technology—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1)(a) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the sectoral software goods, or sectoral software and technology were for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Financial services and funds relating to sectoral software goods and sectoral software and technology</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27U.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide, to a person connected with Iran, financial services in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement whose object or effect is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the export of sectoral software goods,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect supply or delivery of sectoral software goods,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">directly or indirectly making sectoral software goods or sectoral software and technology available to a person,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span goods or sectoral software and technology.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly make funds available to a person connected with Iran in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement mentioned in paragraph (1).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide financial services or funds in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement whose object or effect is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect supply of delivery of sectoral software goods to a place in Iran,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">directly or indirectly making sectoral software goods or sectoral software and technology available—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p software and technology—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a place in Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision of technical assistance relating to sectoral software goods or sectoral software and technology—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for use in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) to (3) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in any of paragraphs (1) to (3) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span (2) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (3) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the financial services or funds (as the case may be) were provided in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement mentioned in that paragraph.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Brokering services: non-UK activity relating to sectoral software goods and sectoral software and technology</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27V.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide brokering services in relation to an arrangement (“arrangement A”) whose object or effect is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the transfer of sectoral software and technology from a place in a third country—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a place in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision, in a non-UK country, of technical assistance relating Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision, in a non-UK country, of financial services—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to a person connected with Iran, where arrangement A, or any other arrangement in connection with which arrangement A is entered into, is an arrangement mentioned in regulation 27U(1), or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">where arrangement A, or any other arrangement in connection with which arrangement A is entered into, is an arrangement mentioned in regulation 27U(3);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">directly or indirectly making funds available, in a non-UK country, to a person connected with Iran, where arrangement A, or any other arrangement in connection with which arrangement A is entered into, is an class="LegAmendingText">(f)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect provision of funds from a non-UK country, where arrangement A, or any other arrangement in connection with which arrangement A is entered into, is an arrangement mentioned in regulation 27U(3).</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with that offence (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the brokering services were provided in relation to an arrangement described in that paragraph.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation, “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">non-UK country</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a country that is not the United Kingdom.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-f">(f)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">for the heading to Chapter 2, substitute <span class="LegAmendingText">“Imports and related activities: certain goods and technology”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-g">(g)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">before regulation 28, insert—</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Interpretation of Chapter 2</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">27W.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">In this Chapter—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">relevant goods and technology</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">military goods;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">missile-list goods;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(c)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">nuclear-list goods;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(d)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">other restricted goods;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(e)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">military technology;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(f)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">missile-list technology;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(g)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">nuclear-list technology;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(h)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">other restricted technology;</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">relevant technology</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">military had no reasonable cause to suspect that the goods originated in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the goods were located in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Supply and delivery of oil and petroleum products</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText financial services or funds (as the case may be) were provided in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement mentioned in that paragraph.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Brokering services relating to petrochemicals</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">31U.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide brokering services in relation to any arrangement described in regulation 31T(1).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with that offence (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the brokering services were provided in relation to an arrangement mentioned in that paragraph.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearChapter"></div><h4 class="LegChapter"><span class="LegChapterNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Chapter 2D</span></span><span class="LegChapterTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Imports and related activities: natural gas</span></span></h4><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Import of natural gas</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">31V.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The import of natural gas which is consigned from Iran is prohibited.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The import of natural gas which originates in Iran is prohibited.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) and (2) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Acquisition of natural gas</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">31W.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly acquire natural gas—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">which originates in Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">which is located in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(a) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the natural gas originated in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the natural gas was located in Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Supply and delivery of natural gas to a third country</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">31X.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly supply or deliver natural gas from a place in Iran to a third country.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the supply or delivery was from a place in Iran, whether directly or indirectly.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Technical assistance relating to natural gas</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">31Y.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide technical assistance relating to—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the import of natural gas which—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">originates in Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">is consigned from Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect acquisition of natural gas which—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">originates in Iran, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">is located in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect supply or delivery of natural gas from a place in Iran to a third country.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes any of the prohibitions in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1)(a), to show that the person did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the technical assistance related to an import described in that paragraph;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b), to show that the person did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the technical assistance related to an acquisition described in that paragraph;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1)(c), to show that the person did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the technical assistance related to a supply or delivery described in that paragraph.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Financial services and funds relating to natural gas</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">31Z.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide financial services or funds in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement whose object or effect is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the import of natural gas which—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect acquisition of natural gas which—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">is located in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the direct or indirect supply of natural gas from a place in Iran to a third country.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span were provided in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement mentioned in that paragraph.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Brokering services relating to natural gas</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">31Z1.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span
