class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with that offence (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the brokering services were provided in relation to an arrangement mentioned in that paragraph.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-7-n">(n)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after regulation 33, insert—</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Banknotes and coinage</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">33A.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The export of restricted banknotes or coinage to, or for the benefit of, the Central Bank of Iran is prohibited.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">supply or deliver restricted banknotes or coinage from a third country to, or for the benefit of, the Central Bank of Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">make banknotes or coinage available to, or for the benefit of, the Central Bank of Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) and (2) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (2) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (2)(a) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person to whom, or for whose benefit, the banknotes or coinage were supplied or delivered was the Central Bank of Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (2)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person to whom, or for whose benefit, the banknotes or coinage were made available was the Central Bank of Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Making available certain vessels</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">33B.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly make available a vessel designed or modified for the transport or storage of oil and petroleum products—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for the benefit of, or for use in, Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(a) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the person was connected with Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1)(b) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the vessel was for the benefit of, or for use in, Iran.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation, “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">vessel</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any thing falling within commodity code 8901 90 (other vessels for the transport of goods and other vessels for the transport of both persons and goods).</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Technical assistance relating to certain tankers and cargo vessels</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">33C.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide technical assistance relating to a tanker or cargo vessel to, or for the benefit of, a person connected with Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">provide financial services, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">make funds available,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement whose object or effect is the provision of technical assistance referred to in paragraph (1).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) and (2) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraphs (1) or (2) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the technical assistance was provided to, or for the benefit of, a person connected to Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (2) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the financial services or funds (as the case may be) were provided in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement mentioned in paragraph (2).</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation, “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">tanker or cargo vessel</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any thing which falls within the following commodity codes—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">8901 2010 (seagoing tankers);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">8901 30 90 (other refrigerated vessels);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">8901 90 00 (other seagoing vessels for the transport of goods and other vessels for the transport of both persons and goods);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(f)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">8901 9090 00 (other vessels for the transport of goods and other vessels for the transport of both persons and goods).</span></span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Technical assistance relating to ships</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">33D.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide technical assistance relating to a ship to, or for the benefit of, a designated person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the technical assistance was provided to, or for the benefit of, a designated person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation, “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">designated person</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a person designated under regulation 5 (power to designate persons) for the purposes of this regulation.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Provision of services relating to specified ships</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">33E.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide in relation to a specified ship—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">brokering services;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">chartering services;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">crew services;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">financial services or funds.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">operating services;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(f)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">technical assistance;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide services relating to the acquisition, sale, transfer or supply of a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) and (2) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) or (2) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the ship in relation to which the assistance, services or funds (as the case may be) were provided was a specified ship;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (2) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the ship in relation to whose acquisition, sale, transfer or supply the services were provided was a specified ship.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">chartering services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means services associated with the chartering of a ship;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">crew services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means services associated with the manning of a ship or the provision of a crew or members of a crew for a ship;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">operating services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means services associated with the operation of a ship.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Procurement of services relating to specified ships</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">33F.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly procure services relating to a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation, services relating to a specified ship include, in particular, services involving the use of that specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes the prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the ship in relation to which the services were procured was a specified ship.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS
