id="regulation-7-q">(q)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">omit regulation 34(3).</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Aircraft and ships</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-8">8.</span>  After Part 5 (Trade), insert—&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h4 class="LegPartFirst"><span class="LegPartNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>Part 5A</span></span><span class="LegPartTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Aircraft</span></span></h4><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Movement of aircraft</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36A.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">An Iranian aircraft must not land in the United Kingdom.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may direct the CAA to—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">refuse permission under article 250 of the ANO in respect of an Iranian aircraft,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">refuse permission under article 252 of the ANO in respect of an Iranian aircraft,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">revoke any permission granted under article 250 of the ANO in respect of an Iranian aircraft, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">revoke any permission granted under article 252 of the ANO in respect of an Iranian aircraft.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">An airport operator may direct the operator or pilot in command of an Iranian aircraft—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to take off, or to require the aircraft to take off, from an airport the airport operator manages, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">not to land, or not to permit the aircraft to land, at an airport the airport operator manages.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may direct an airport operator to give a direction under paragraph (4).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">air cargo services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a service for the carriage by air of cargo, including mail and animals;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">designated person</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a person who is designated under regulation 5 (power to designate persons) for the purposes of this regulation;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Iranian aircraft</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means an aircraft used exclusively for the provision of air cargo services which is—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">owned, chartered or operated by—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegRomanList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel5NoAmend LegListItemNo">(i)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a designated person, or</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel5NoAmend LegListItemNo">(ii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel5Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a person connected with Iran, or</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">registered in Iran.</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Directions under regulation 36A</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36B.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (2) to (4) apply in relation to a direction given under regulation 36A (movement of aircraft).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person to whom a direction is given has a duty to comply with it.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A direction may be of indefinite duration or a defined duration.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who gives a direction may vary, revoke or suspend it at any time.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A direction under regulation 36A(5) may be given to any airport operator or to airport operators generally.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Any directions given by the Secretary of State under regulation 36A may make different provision for different purposes.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(7)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Any directions given under regulation 36A(3) to (5) are subject to the exceptions in regulation 39C (aircraft: exceptions from prohibitions).</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Directions under regulation 36A: supplementary</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36C.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Where a direction is given under regulation 36A(3)(c) or (d)—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to the extent that the direction conflicts with the requirements of article 255 of the ANO (revocation of permissions etc.), those requirements are to be disregarded, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">article 255(4) of the ANO does not apply in relation to the revocation which is the subject of the direction.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Where a direction is given under regulation 36A which conflicts with a permission under article 250 of the ANO, the permission is to be disregarded.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">insofar as a direction under regulation 36A conflicts with the requirements of section 93 of the Transport Act 2000</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00009" title="Go to footnote 9" id="Backf00009">9</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText"> or an order under section 94 of that Act, the direction is to be disregarded.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">insofar as a direction under regulation 36A conflicts with the requirements of an enactment other than section 93 of the Transport Act 2000 or an order under section 94 of that Act, the requirements are to be disregarded.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may notify a person that the existence of a direction under regulation 36A, any part of the content of the direction, or anything done under the direction, is to be treated as confidential.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not disclose any information if the Secretary of State notifies that person under paragraph (5) that the information is to be treated as confidential.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Aircraft offences</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36D.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">If the prohibition in regulation 36A(1) is contravened by the landing of an Iranian aircraft (as defined by regulation 36A(6)), the operator and pilot in command of the Iranian aircraft commit an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">It is an offence for a person to whom a direction is given under regulation 36A(4) to fail to comply with the direction.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">It is an offence for an airport operator to fail, without reasonable excuse, to comply with a direction given by the Secretary of State under regulation 36A(5).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes the prohibition in regulation 36C(6) commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Interpretation of Part 5A</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36E.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this Part—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">the ANO</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means the Air Navigation Order 2016</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00010" title="Go to footnote 10" id="Backf00010">10</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">beneficial interest</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any beneficial interest, however arising (whether held by trustee or nominee or arising under a contract or otherwise), other than an interest held by any person as mortgagee.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of regulation 36A(6)(a), an aircraft is “owned” by a person if—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the legal title to the aircraft, or to any share in the aircraft, is vested in the person, or a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of regulation 7) by that person, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the person, or a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of regulation 7) by that person, has a beneficial interest in the aircraft or in any share in the aircraft,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">and the reference to a legal title or other interest includes one held jointly with any other person or persons.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Any expression used in this Part and in section 6 of the Act (aircraft sanctions) has the same meaning in this Part as it has in that section.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h4 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Part 5B</span></span><span class="LegPartTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Ships</span></span></h4><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Prohibition on chartering or operating specified ships</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36F.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not charter a specified ship, if the person knows or has reasonable cause to suspect, that the ship is a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not operate a specified ship, if the person knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that the ship is a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) and (2) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) or (2) commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Prohibition on port entry</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36G.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not provide a ship to which this paragraph applies with access to a port in the United Kingdom, if the person knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that the ship is a ship to which this paragraph applies.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The master or pilot of a ship to which this paragraph applies must not cause or permit the ship to enter a port in the United Kingdom if the master or pilot knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that the ship is a ship to which this paragraph applies.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) and (2) are subject to Part 6 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) or (2) commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) and (2) apply to—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a ship owned, controlled, chartered or operated by a designated person, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a specified ship.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Directions prohibiting port entry</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36H.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A port barring direction may be given to the master or pilot of a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A port barring direction may be given by—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the Secretary of State, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a harbour authority.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may direct a harbour authority to take such steps as are reasonably practicable to secure that a ship mentioned in a port barring direction does not enter a port or ports in the United Kingdom specified in the direction, or any port in the United Kingdom.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">It is an offence for a person to whom a direction is given under this regulation to fail to comply with the direction.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may notify a person that the existence of a port barring direction, any part of the content of the direction, or anything done under the direction, is to be treated as confidential.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">It is an offence for a person to disclose information if the Secretary of State has notified that person under paragraph (5) that the information is to be treated as confidential.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(7)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation, a “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">port barring direction</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a direction prohibiting a ship from entering a port or ports in the United Kingdom specified in the direction, or any port in the United Kingdom.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Movement of ships</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36I.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A port entry direction or a movement direction may be given by the Secretary of State to the master or pilot of—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a ship owned, controlled, chartered or operated by a designated person, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a specified ship.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may direct a harbour authority to take such steps as are reasonably practicable to secure that a ship mentioned in paragraph (1)—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">proceeds to or enters a port specified in the direction,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">leaves a port specified in the direction,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">proceeds to a place specified in the direction, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">remains where it is.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">It is an offence for a person to whom a direction is given under this regulation to fail to comply with the direction.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may notify a person that the existence of a port entry direction or a movement direction, any part of the content of the direction, or anything done under the direction, is to be treated as confidential.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">It is an offence for a person to disclose information if the Secretary of State has notified that person under paragraph (4) that the information is to be treated as confidential.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">movement direction</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a direction requiring a ship—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to leave a port specified in the direction,</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to proceed to a place specified in the direction, or</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(c)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">to remain where it is;</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">port entry direction</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a direction requiring a ship to proceed to or enter a port specified in the direction.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Detention of ships</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36J.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A detention direction may be given to the master of a ship referred to in paragraph (3) by—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the Secretary of State, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a harbour authority.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A detention direction under paragraph (1)(b) may only be given by a harbour authority to the master of a specified ship if the harbour authority has received a direction from the Secretary of State in accordance with paragraph (3)(b).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may direct a harbour authority to give a detention direction to the master of—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a ship owned, controlled, chartered or operated by a designated person, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a specified ship.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">detention direction</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a direction requiring the detention of a ship at a port or anchorage in the United Kingdom.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A detention direction given in relation to a ship must—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">be in writing,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">be delivered to the master of the ship by the person who detains the ship,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">state the grounds on which the ship is detained, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">state that—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is given under this regulation, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">any requirements imposed by the direction must be complied with.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (7) applies if—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the ship is not a British ship</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00011" title="Go to footnote 11" id="Backf00011">11</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">there is in the United Kingdom a consular officer for the country to which the ship belongs.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(7)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A copy of the detention direction must be sent as soon as practicable to the nearest consular officer for the country to which the ship belongs.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(8)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Section 284(1), (2), (2A), (2B), (3) and (8) of the Merchant Shipping Act 1995</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00012" title="Go to footnote 12" id="Backf00012">12</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText"> (enforcing detention of ship) applies in the case of detention under a detention direction as it applies in the case of detention authorised or ordered by that Act, but as if—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">any reference in that section to a notice of detention were to the detention direction, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the reference in subsection (2A) of that section to a direction given under subsection (1A)(a)</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00013" title="Go to footnote 13" id="Backf00013">13</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText"> of that section were to any requirement imposed by the detention direction.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(9)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation, “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">consular officer</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”, in relation to a foreign country, means the officer recognised by His Majesty as a consular officer of that foreign country.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Registration of ships in the United Kingdom</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36K.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Registrar</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00014" title="Go to footnote 14" id="Backf00014">14</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText"> must refuse to register a ship if, on the basis of the information given to the Registrar by the Secretary of State or accompanying the application for registration, the ship appears to the Registrar to be—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">owned, controlled, chartered or operated by a designated person, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a specified ship.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may direct the Registrar to terminate the registration of—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a ship that is owned, controlled, chartered or operated by a designated person, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a specified ship.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation, any reference to registering a ship is to registering the ship in the register of British ships maintained by the Registrar.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Specification of ships</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36L.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may specify ships within the meaning of section 7 of the Act for any of the following purposes—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 33E (provision of services relating to specified ships);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 33F (procurement of services relating to specified ships);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 36F (prohibition on chartering or operating specified ships);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 36G (prohibition on port entry);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 36H (directions prohibiting port entry);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(f)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 36I (movement of ships);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(g)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 36J (detention of ships);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(h)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 36K (registration of ships in the United Kingdom).</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State must specify a ship by its International Maritime Organization number or, where it is not reasonably practicable to identify it by that number, by any other means that the Secretary of State considers appropriate.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State may not specify a ship unless the Secretary of State has reasonable grounds to suspect that the ship is, has been, or is likely to be, involved in a relevant activity.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation, a ship is “involved in a relevant activity” if the ship is used for any activity whose object or effect is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">engaging in, providing support for, promoting or facilitating a relevant nuclear activity;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">making available funds or economic resources that could contribute to a relevant nuclear activity;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the supply to Iran of restricted goods, restricted technology, dual-use goods or dual-use technology;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the contravention or circumvention of any provision of these Regulations.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The activities described in paragraph (4) include carrying—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">goods for use in, or otherwise related to, a relevant nuclear activity;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">oil and petroleum products that originated in Iran—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from a place in Iran to a third country;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from a third country to a place in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(iii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from one third country to another third country;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">natural gas that originated in Iran—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from a place in Iran to a third country;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from a third country to a place in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(iii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from one third country to another third country;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">restricted goods, restricted technology, dual-use goods or dual-use technology—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from a place in Iran to a third country;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from a third country to a place in Iran;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(iii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from one third country to another third country.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purpose of paragraph (5), “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">carrying</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” includes any transfer of the goods or technology concerned between ships on which those goods or technology are being carried as mentioned in that paragraph.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(7)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">dual-use goods</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">any thing for the time being specified in Annex I of the Dual-Use Regulation, other than any thing which is dual-use technology, and</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">any tangible storage medium on which dual-use technology is recorded or from which it can be derived;</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">dual-use technology</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any thing for the time being specified in Annex I of the Dual-Use Regulation which is described as software or technology;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">natural gas</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any thing specified in Schedule 1E (natural gas);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">oil and petroleum products</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any thing which is specified in Schedule 1F (oil and petroleum products);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">relevant nuclear activity</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in regulation 6(4);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">restricted goods</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in regulation 20;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">restricted technology</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in regulation 20.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Notification and publicity where specification power used</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36M.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (2) applies where the Secretary of State—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">has specified a ship under regulation 36L(1) (specification of ships), or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">has, by virtue of section 26 of the Act, revoked a specification made under that regulation.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">must without delay take such steps as are reasonably practicable to inform such persons as the Secretary of State considers appropriate of the specification or revocation, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">except where one or more of the restricted publicity conditions is met, must take steps to publicise the specification or revocation generally.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The “restricted publicity conditions” are that the Secretary of State considers that disclosure of the specification or revocation should be restricted—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in the interests of national security or international relations,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for reasons connected with the prevention or detection of serious crime in the United Kingdom or elsewhere, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in the interests of justice.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (5) applies if—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">when a specification is made one or more of the restricted publicity conditions is met, but</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">at any time when the specification has effect, it becomes the case that none of the restricted publicity conditions is met.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The Secretary of State must take steps to publicise the specification generally.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Directions under this Part: general</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36N.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (2) to (4) apply in relation to a direction given under this Part.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person to whom a direction is given has a duty to comply with it.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A direction may be of indefinite duration or a defined duration.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who gives a direction may vary, revoke or suspend it at any time.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A direction under regulation 36I(2) (movement of ships) may be given to any harbour authority or to harbour authorities generally.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Interpretation of Part 5B</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">36O.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this Part, a ship is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“owned” by a person if—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the legal title to the ship, or to any share in the ship, is vested in the person, or in a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of regulation 7) by that person, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the person, or a person who is owned or controlled directly or indirectly (within the meaning of regulation 7) by that person, has a beneficial interest in the ship or in any share in the ship;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“controlled” by a person who is able to take decisions about its operation, including (but not limited to) decisions about the route the ship may take and the appointment of master or crew.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Any reference in this Part to a legal title or other interest includes one held jointly with any other person or persons.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this Part—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">beneficial interest</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any beneficial interest, however arising (whether held by trustee or nominee or arising under a contract or otherwise), other than an interest held by any person as mortgagee;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">designated person</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a person who is designated under regulation 5 for the purposes of any of regulations 36F, 36G, 36H, 36I, 36J and 36K;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">specified ship</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a ship specified by the Secretary of State under regulation 36L (specification of ships).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Any expression used in this Part and in section 7 of the Act (shipping sanctions) has the same meaning in this Part as it has in that section.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Exceptions and licences</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-9">9.</span>  In Part 6 (Exceptions and licences)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-9-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after regulation 37ZA (finance: humanitarian exception), insert—</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Finance: exceptions relating to insurance and reinsurance services</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">37ZB.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibition in regulation 17C(1) (insurance and reinsurance services) is not contravened by—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the provision of compulsory or third party insurance to an Iranian person located in the United Kingdom;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the provision of insurance for a diplomatic mission or consular post of Iran in the United Kingdom.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibition in regulation 17C(1), insofar as it prohibits the provision of insurance or reinsurance services to persons acting on behalf of, or at the direction of, a person connected with Iran, is not contravened by—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the provision of insurance or reinsurance services to an individual acting on behalf of or at the direction of a person connected with Iran, provided that—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i )</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the insurance or reinsurance is for the individual's personal use, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the individual is not a designated person;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the provision of insurance or reinsurance services to a person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) who is the owner of a ship or aircraft which is chartered, or a vehicle which is hired, by a person connected with Iran, provided that P is not a designated person.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">consular post</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in regulation 37D(5);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">designated person</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a person who is designated under regulation 5 for the purposes of regulations 12 to 17;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">diplomatic mission</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in regulation 37D(5);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Iranian person</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a national of Iran, or</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a body incorporated or constituted under the law of Iran.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-9-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in regulation 37A (exception for authorised conduct in a relevant country), in paragraph (1), after “17 (asset-freeze etc)” insert <span class="LegAmendingText">“, regulations 17A to 17D (investment, financial services and financial markets)”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-9-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after regulation 37A, insert—</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Exception for emergencies in certain cases</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">37B.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions specified in paragraph (2) are not contravened by any act done by a person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”), where P provides justification to the Secretary of State within the relevant period that the act is an act dealing with an emergency.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) applies to the prohibitions in—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 17C(1) (insurance and reinsurance services), insofar as it applies to insurance and reinsurance for the maritime transportation of oil and petroleum products;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Chapter 1B of Part 5 (Exports and related activities: energy-related goods and energy-related technology);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Chapter 1C of Part 5 (Sectoral software goods and sectoral software and technology);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Chapter 2A of Part 5 (Imports and related activities: gold, precious metals and diamonds);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Chapter 2B of Part 5 (Imports and related activities: oil and petroleum products);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(f)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Chapter 2C of Part 5 (Imports and related activities: petrochemicals);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(g)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Chapter 2D of Part 5 (Imports and related activities: natural gas);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(h)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 33C (technical assistance relating to certain tankers and cargo vessels);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 33D (technical assistance relating to ships);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(j)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 33E (provision of services relating to specified ships);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(k)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 33F (procurement of services relating to specified ships);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(l)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 36F(2) (prohibition on operating specified ships).</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">an act dealing with an emergency</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means an act assisting with the urgent prevention or mitigation of an event likely to have a serious and significant impact on human health or safety, infrastructure or the environment;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">relevant period</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”, in relation to an act, means the period of 5 working days beginning with the day on which the act is done.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Trade: exceptions in relation to energy-related goods and energy-related technology</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">37C.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions in regulations 27M (financial services and funds relating to energy-related goods and energy-related technology) and 27N (brokering services: non-UK activity relating to energy-related goods and energy-related technology) are not contravened in relation to the provision of insurance or reinsurance to a relevant person where the provision of such insurance or reinsurance is not connected with the energy sector in Iran.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions in Chapter 1B of Part 5 (exports and related activities: energy-related goods and energy-related technology) and Chapter 1C of Part 5 (sectoral software goods and sectoral software and technology) are not contravened by a relevant activity that is necessary for the purposes of a UK petroleum project.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">energy-related goods</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in regulation 34(1) (interpretation of Part 5);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">energy-related technology</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in regulation 34(1);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">relevant activity</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any activity which would, in the absence of this regulation, contravene the prohibitions in Chapters 1B and 1C of Part 5;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">relevant person</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a person who is not a person connected with Iran;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">UK petroleum project</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means an oil or gas exploration or production project that is wholly or partially located within—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the United Kingdom;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">waters adjacent to the United Kingdom up to the seaward limits of the territorial sea, or the seabed and subsoil below them;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(c)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">areas from time to time designated under section 1(7) of the Continental Shelf Act 1964 (designation of areas of continental shelf)</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00015" title="Go to footnote 15" id="Backf00015">15</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">, and any waters within the limits of such areas.</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Trade: exceptions relating to sectoral software goods and sectoral software and technology</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">37D.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions in Chapter 1C of Part 5 (sectoral software goods and sectoral software and technology) are not contravened by any act done by a person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) in satisfaction of an obligation to a person connected with Iran where that act is to discharge, or comply with, UK statutory or regulatory obligations, such obligations not arising under contract, provided that P notifies the Secretary of State of that act before the end of the period of 12 months beginning with the first day on which the act is done.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions in Chapter 1C of Part 5 are not contravened by any act done by a person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) in satisfaction of an obligation arising under a contract concluded before 8th September 2026, or an ancillary contract necessary for the satisfaction of such a contract, provided that—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the act is carried out before the end of 7th March 2027, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q notifies the Secretary of State of that act, before or after the act is carried out, before the end of 7th March 2027.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions in Chapter 1C of Part 5 are not contravened by activities relating to sectoral software goods, or sectoral software and technology, where those goods or software and technology are of a non-commercial nature or for personal use.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions in Chapter 1C of Part 5 are not contravened by activities relating to sectoral software goods, or sectoral software and technology, which is necessary for the official purposes of a diplomatic mission or consular post in Iran, or an international organisation enjoying immunities in accordance with international law.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">consular post</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the same meaning as in the Vienna Convention on Consular Relations done at Vienna on 24 April 1963</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00016" title="Go to footnote 16" id="Backf00016">16</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">, and any reference to the functions of a consular post is to be read in accordance with that Convention;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">diplomatic mission</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”, and any reference to the functions of a diplomatic mission, is to be read in accordance with the Vienna Convention on Diplomatic Relations done at Vienna on 18 April 1961</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00017" title="Go to footnote 17" id="Backf00017">17</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">;</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Trade: exceptions in relation to oil and petroleum products, petrochemicals and natural gas</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">37E.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions in Chapters 2B, 2C and 2D of Part 5 are not contravened by any relevant activity in relation to any oil and petroleum products, petrochemicals or natural gas which is necessary for the official purposes of a diplomatic mission or consular post in Iran, or an international organisation enjoying immunities in accordance with international law.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">consular post</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” and “diplomatic mission” have the meanings given in regulation 37D(4);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">natural gas</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in regulation 34(1);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">oil and petroleum products</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in regulation 34(1);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">petrochemicals</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the meaning given in regulation 34(1);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">relevant activity</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any activity which would, in the absence of this regulation, contravene any of the prohibitions in Chapter 2B, 2C or 2D of Part 5.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Trade: exception in respect of the acquisition of goods</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">37F.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions specified in paragraph (3) do not apply in relation to relevant goods located in the United Kingdom or the Isle of Man having been lawfully imported there.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions specified in paragraph (3)(e) to (l) do not apply to a United Kingdom national in Iran engaging in any activity subject to any of those prohibitions where—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the relevant goods are located in Iran, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">those relevant goods are for the purposes of personal use, in Iran, by that United Kingdom national, or their immediate family members.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions specified in this paragraph are those in—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 31E(1) (acquisition of gold, precious metals and 31H(1)(b) (financial services or funds relating to the acquisition of gold, precious metals or diamonds);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 31I(1) (brokering services relating to gold, precious metals or diamonds) insofar as the prohibition relates to arrangements specified in regulation 31H(1)(b);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 31K(1) (acquisition of oil and petroleum products);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(f)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(g)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 31N(1)(b) (financial services and funds relating to the acquisition of oil and petroleum products);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(k)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 31T(1)(b) (financial regulation 31Z(1)(b).</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegAmendingText">” means—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a wife or husband;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard
