LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a child or step-child;</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(e)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a grandchild;</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">relevant goods</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any thing specified in Schedules 1B (gold, precious metals and diamonds), 1E (natural gas), 1F (oil and petroleum products) and 1H (petrochemicals) as applicable.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Trade: exceptions in relation to maritime goods and maritime technology</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">37G.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions in Chapters 1 (Exports and related activities) to 3 (Other trade restrictions in relation to Iran) of Part 5 (Trade)—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">insofar as they relate to maritime goods, do not apply to the export, supply or delivery, acquisition or making available of such goods, or to the provision of technical assistance, financial services, funds or brokering services, necessary for a purpose specified in paragraph (2);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">insofar as they relate to maritime technology, do not apply to the acquisition, transfer or making available of such technology, or to the provision of technical assistance, financial services, funds or brokering services, necessary for a purpose specified in paragraph (2).</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The purposes are—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">non-military use by a non-military end-user;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">addressing a health emergency;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the urgent prevention or mitigation of an event likely to have a serious and significant impact on human health and safety or on the environment; or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">providing a response to a natural disaster.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation, “maritime goods” and “maritime technology” have the meanings given in regulation 34 (interpretation of Part 5).<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-9-d">(d)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after regulation 39 (exception relating to services provided to certain ships and aircraft), insert—</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Trade: exceptions in relation to vessels</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">39A.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of regulation 33B (making available certain vessels), the removal of a vessel from the United Kingdom to Iran is not making available a vessel designed or modified for the transport or storage of oil and petroleum products, provided that the following conditions are met—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the vessel is removed under its own power;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the removal of the vessel is not for the purpose of—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a transfer of ownership of the vessel or any of its component parts, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a change of the operator of the vessel.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation, “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">operator</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”, in relation to a vessel, means the person having the management of the vessel.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Trade: exceptions relating to specified ships</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">39B.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions specified in paragraph (2) are not contravened by conduct that is necessary to protect or avoid endangering—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the safety of any ship, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the life of any person.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) applies to the prohibitions—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in regulation 33E (provision of services relating to specified ships);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in regulation 33F (procurement of services relating to specified ships).</span></span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Aircraft: exceptions from prohibitions</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">39C.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibition in regulation 36A(1) (movement of aircraft) is not contravened by the landing of an Iranian aircraft in the United Kingdom—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">if failing to land would endanger the lives of persons on board or the safety of the aircraft;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">if the aircraft is engaged in non-scheduled international air services and is exercising its right to land in the United Kingdom for non-traffic purposes in accordance with Article 5 of the Chicago Convention;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">if the aircraft is engaged in scheduled international air services and is exercising its right to land in the United Kingdom for non-traffic purposes in accordance with section 1 of Article 1 of the IASTA.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A direction given under regulation 36A(3) to (5) is not contravened by conduct that is necessary to—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">avoid endangering the lives of persons on board or the safety of the aircraft,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">protect the safety of another aircraft, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">protect the safety of persons on the ground.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">the Chicago Convention</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means the Convention on International Civil Aviation and its Annexes, signed at Chicago on 7th December 1944</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00018" title="Go to footnote 18" id="Backf00018">18</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">the IASTA</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means the International Air Services Transit Agreement signed at Chicago on 7th December 1944</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00019" title="Go to footnote 19" id="Backf00019">19</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Iranian aircraft</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” has the same meaning as in regulation 36A(6);</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">non-traffic purposes</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” is to be read in accordance with Article 96(d) of the Chicago Convention.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Ships: exceptions from prohibitions on port entry</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">39D.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibition in regulation 36G(1) (prohibition on port entry) is not contravened by providing a ship with access to a port if—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a port entry direction has been given in relation to the ship under regulation 36I(1) (movement of ships), or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the access is needed by the ship in a case of emergency.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibition in regulation 36G(2) is not contravened by the entry into port of a ship if—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a port entry direction has been given in relation to the ship under regulation 36I(1), or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the entry is needed by the ship in a case of emergency.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Ships: exceptions from prohibition on operating specified ships</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">39E.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person does not contravene the prohibition in regulation 36F(2) (operating specified ships) by conduct in the United Kingdom or in the territorial sea where the person—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the person’s presence in the United Kingdom or in the territorial sea arises directly from an exercise of either—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the right of transit passage,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in accordance with the provisions of the United Nations Convention on the Law of the Sea</span>(<a class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person does not contravene the prohibition in regulation 36F(2) by conduct that is necessary to protect or avoid endangering—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the safety of any ship, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the life of any person.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-9-e">(e)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in regulation 40(1) (Treasury licences), after “17 (asset-freeze etc)”, insert <span class="LegAmendingText">“and regulations 17A to 17D (investments, accounts and corresponding banking relationships, insurance and reinsurance and sale or purchase of bonds)”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-9-f">(f)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after regulation 41 (trade licences), insert—</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Aircraft licences</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">41A.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibition in regulation 36A(1) (prohibition on landing) does not apply to anything done under the authority of a licence issued by the Secretary of State under this regulation.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Ships licences</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">41B.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions in regulation 36F(1) and (2) (prohibition on chartering or operating specified ships) do not apply to anything done under the authority of a licence issued by the Secretary of State under this regulation.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-9-g">(g)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in regulation 42(1), for “and trade licences” substitute <span class="LegAmendingText">“, trade licences, aircraft licences and ships licences”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" class="LegAmendingText">Aircraft: licensing offences</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">44A.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) commits an offence if P knowingly or recklessly—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">provides information that is false in a material respect, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">provides or produces a document that is not what it purports to be,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for the purpose of obtaining an aircraft licence (whether for P or anyone else).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who purports to act under the authority of an aircraft licence but who fails to comply with any condition of the aircraft licence commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A licence in respect of which an offence under paragraph (1) has been committed is to be treated as void from the time at which it was issued.</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Ships: licensing offences</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">44B.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) commits an offence if P knowingly or recklessly—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">provides information that is false in a material respect, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">provides or produces a document that is not what it purports to be,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">A person who purports to act under the authority of a ships licence but who fails to comply with any condition of the ships licence commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A licence in respect of which an offence under paragraph (1) has been committed is to be treated as void from the time at which it was issued.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Disclosure of information</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-10">10.</span>  In regulation 54 (disclosure of information), in paragraph (2)(d)(ii), for the words from “a prohibition” to the end, substitute <span class="LegAmendingText">“any prohibition in Part 5 (trade) on imports or exports”</span>.&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Enforcement</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in paragraph (1), in the opening words, after “Part 3 (Finance)” insert <span class="LegAmendingText">“, regulation 36D(1), (2) or (3) (aircraft offences), 36F (prohibition on chartering or operating a specified ship)”</span>, 36G(4) (prohibition on port entry), 36H(4) (directions prohibiting port entry) or 36I(3) (movement of ships: directions),”;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-1-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after paragraph (2), insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(2A)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who commits an offence under regulation 36D(4) (disclosure of information), 36H(6) (directions prohibiting port entry: confidentiality) or 36I(5) (movement of ships: confidentiality) is liable—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">on summary conviction in England and Wales, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 6 months or a fine (or both);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">on summary conviction in Scotland, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 6 months or a fine not exceeding level 5 on the standard scale (or both);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">on summary conviction in Northern Ireland, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding 6 months or a fine not exceeding level 5 on the standard scale (or both).<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-11-1-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in paragraph (3), in the opening words, after “44 (trade: licensing offences),” insert <span class="LegAmendingText">“44A (aircraft: licensing offences), 44B (ships: licensing offences),”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In regulation 58(1) (jurisdiction to try offences), after “regulation 43 (finance: licensing offences)” insert <span class="LegAmendingText">“, regulation 44A (aircraft: licensing offences), 44B (ships: licensing offences)”</span>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In regulation 61 (trade enforcement: application of CEMA), in paragraph (3B), after sub-paragraph (e), insert—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(f)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 27B (supply and delivery of gold, precious metals and diamonds to Iran), insofar as the offence does not relate to the import or export of 1I</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-13">13.</span>  After Schedule 1, insert—&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearSchedule"></div><p class="LegArticleRef"><span class="LegAmendingText">Regulation 34(1)</span></p><h4 class="LegScheduleFirst"><span class="LegScheduleNo LegHeadingRefAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>Schedule 1a</span></span><span class="LegTitleBlockTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Energy-related goods and energy-related technology</span></span></h4><div class="LegClearPartFirst"></div><h5 class="LegPartFirst"><span class="LegPartNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Part 1</span></span><span class="LegPartTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Introduction</span></span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">1.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">In this Schedule, “development”, “production”, “required”, “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">software</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”, “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">technology</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” and “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">use</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” have the meanings given to them in the Dual-Use Regulation.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">2.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of Sections 1E, 2E and 3D in Part 3 of this Schedule, technology is not restricted as energy-related technology if it is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the minimum necessary for—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the installation, operation, maintenance or repair of any goods which are restricted goods as defined by regulation 20, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">patent applications, including information for patent application,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in the public domain, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">basic scientific research.</span></span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h5 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo LegAmend"><span class="LegPartTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Energy-related equipment and technology</span></span></h5><h6 class="LegChapterFirst"><span class="LegChapterNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Chapter 1</span></span><span class="LegChapterTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Exploration and production of crude oil and natural gas</span></span></h6><h6 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Section 1A: Equipment</span></h6><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">4.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Geophysical survey equipment, vehicles, vessels and aircraft specially designed or adapted to acquire data for oil and gas exploration and specially designed components therefor.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">5.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Sensors specially designed for downhole well operations in oil and gas wells, including sensors used for measurement whilst drilling and the associated equipment specially designed to acquire and store data from such sensors.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">6.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Drilling equipment designed to drill rock formations, specifically for the purpose of exploring for, or producing, oil, gas and other naturally occurring hydrocarbon materials.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">7.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Drill bits, drill pipes, drill collars, centralisers and other equipment, specially designed for use in and with oil and gas well drilling equipment.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">8.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Drilling wellheads, blowout preventers and Christmas or production trees and the specially designed components thereof, meeting the API and ISO specifications for use with oil and gas wells, and for the purposes of this paragraph—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a “blowout preventer” is a device typically used at ground level (or, if drilling underwater, at the seabed) during drilling to prevent the uncontrolled escape of oil and/or gas from the well;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a “Christmas tree or production tree” is a device typically used to control flow of fluids from the well when it is complete and oil and/or gas production has started;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">API and ISO specifications</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” refers to the American Petroleum Institute specifications 6A, 16A, 17D and 11IW and the International Standards Organisation specifications 10423 and 13533 for blowout preventers, wellhead and Christmas trees for use on oil and/or gas wells.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">9.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Drilling and production platforms for crude oil and natural gas.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">10.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Vessels and barges incorporating drilling and/or petroleum processing equipment used for producing oil, gas and other naturally occurring flammable materials.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">11.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Liquid/gas separators meeting API specification 12J, specially designed to process the production from an oil or gas well, to separate the petroleum liquids from any water and any gas from the liquids, and for these purposes “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">API specification 12J</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” refers to the American Petroleum Institute specification for process design of oil and gas separators and scrubbers.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">12.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Gas compressor with a design pressure of 40 bar (PN 40 and/or ANSI 300) or more and having a suction volume capacity of 300,000 Nm3/h or more, for the initial processing and transmission of natural gas, excluded gas compressors for CNG (Compressed Natural Gas) filling stations, and specially designed components therefor.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">13.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Sub-sea production control equipment and the components thereof meeting API and ISO specifications for use with oil and gas wells.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In sub-paragraph (1), “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">API and ISO specifications</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” refers to the American Petroleum Institute specification 17 F and/or the International Standards Organisation specification 13268 for sub-sea production control systems.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">14.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Pumps, typically high capacity and/or high pressure (in excess of 0.3 m3 per minute and/or 40 bar), specially designed to pump drilling muds and/or cement into oil and gas wells.</span>&#xD;</p><h6 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Section 1B: Test and inspection equipment</span></h6><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span other materials taken from an oil and/or gas well either during or after drilling, or from the initial processing facilities attached thereto.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">17.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Equipment specially designed for collecting and interpreting information about the physical and mechanical condition of an oil and/or gas well, and for determining the in situ properties of the rock and reservoir formation.</span>&#xD;</p><h6 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Section 1C: Materials</span></h6><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">18.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">Drilling mud, drilling mud additives and the components thereof, specially formulated to stabilise oil and gas wells during drilling, to recover drill cuttings to the surface and to lubricate and cool the drilling equipment in the well.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">19.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span specially formulated for use in the cementing of oil and gas wells.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">20.</span></span>  <span designed for the measurement of the weight of vehicles, e.g. weigh bridges</span>&#xD;</p></td></tr><tr id="Legislation-2-5-3-13-3-5-3-7-1-5-3-3-25-3-3-1-0"><td class="LegTDplain" class="LegTDmixedText"><span class="LegAmendingText"> </span></span><p class="LegText"><span class="LegAmendingText">Electrical power generation gas turbines, components and related equipment as follows—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel3No LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3"><span (a)</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h5 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Part is—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span></span>&#xD;</p><a class="LegAnchorID" id="part-3"></a><div class="LegClearPart"></div><h2 class="LegPart"><span class="LegPartNo">Part 3</span><span class="LegPartTitle">Amendment of the 2023 Regulations</span></h2><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst">Amendment of the 2023 Regulations</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-14">14.</span>  The 2023 Regulations are amended in accordance with this Part.&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Interpretation</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-15">15.</span>  In regulation 2 (interpretation), after the definition of “serious human rights violation or abuse” insert—&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">ships licence</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a licence issued by the Secretary of State under regulation 62B (ships licences);<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Application of prohibitions and requirements outside the United Kingdom</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-16">16.</span>  In regulation 3 (application of prohibitions and requirements outside the United Kingdom)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-16-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in paragraph (3)(e), for “or a trade licence” substitute <span class="LegAmendingText">“, a trade licence or a ships licence”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-16-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in paragraph (6)(b), for “or a trade licence” substitute <span class="LegAmendingText">“, a trade licence or a ships licence”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Power to designate persons</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-17">17.</span>  In regulation 5 (power to designate persons)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-17-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in paragraph (1), after sub-paragraph (c), insert— </span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(ca)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 43B (technical assistance relating to ships);<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-17-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in paragraph (2), after sub-paragraph (b), insert— </span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(ba)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 43B (technical assistance relating to ships);<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Ships: technical assistance and the provision and procurement of services</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-18">18.</span>  After regulation 43 (brokering services: non-UK activity relating to goods of strategic concern and technology or strategic concern), insert—&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearChapter"></div><h4 class="LegChapterFirst"><span class="LegChapterNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>Chapter 4A</span></span><span class="LegChapterTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Ships</span></span></h4><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Interpretation of this Chapter</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">43A.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">In this Chapter—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">chartering services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means services associated with the chartering of a ship;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">crew services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means services associated with the manning of a ship or the provision of a crew or members of a crew for a ship;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">operating services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means services associated with the operation of a ship;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">ship</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” includes every description of vessel (including a hovercraft) used in navigation;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">specified ship</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a ship specified by the Secretary of State under regulation 51 (specification of ships).</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Technical assistance relating to ships</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">43B.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide technical assistance relating to a ship to, or for the benefit of, a designated person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 8 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the technical assistance was provided to, or for the benefit of, a designated person.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation, a “</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">designated person</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a person designated under regulation 5 (power to designate persons) for the purposes of this regulation.</span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Provision of services relating to specified ships</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">43C.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide in relation to a specified ship—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">brokering services;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">chartering services;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(c)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">crew services;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">financial services or funds;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(e)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">operating services;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(f)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">technical assistance;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide services relating to the acquisition, sale, transfer or supply of a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) and (2) are subject to Part 8 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) or (2) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the ship in relation to which the assistance, services or funds (as the case may be) were provided was a specified ship;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (2) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">Q</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that Q did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the ship in relation to whose acquisition, sale, transfer or supply the services where provided was a specified ship.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h5 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Procurement of services relating to specified ships</span></h5><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">43D.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly procure services relating to a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation, services relating to a specified ship include, in particular, services involving the use of that specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 8 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes the prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1) (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) to show that P did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the ship in relation to which the services were procured was a specified ship.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Prohibition on chartering or operating specified ships</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-19">19.</span>  In Part 7, before regulation 46 (prohibition on port entry), insert—&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Prohibition on chartering or operating specified ships</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">45A.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not charter a specified ship, if the person knows or has reasonable cause to suspect, that the ship is a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not operate a specified ship, if the person knows, or has reasonable cause to suspect, that the ship is a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) and (2) are subject to Part 8 (Exceptions and licences) (see in particular, regulation 56C (ships: exceptions from prohibitions)).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) or (2) commits an offence.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Registration of ships in the United Kingdom</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-20">20.</span>  In regulation 50 (registration of ships in the United Kingdom), in paragraph (1)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" or”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-20-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after sub-paragraph (a), insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(b)</span></span><span LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">56B.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions specified in paragraph (2) are ships).</span></span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Ships: exceptions from prohibition on operating specified ships</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">56C.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person does not contravene the prohibition in regulation 45A(2) (operating specified ships) by conduct in the United Kingdom or in the territorial sea where the person—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">is not a United Kingdom person, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the person’s presence in the United Kingdom or in the territorial sea arises directly from an exercise of either—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the right of innocent passage, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the right of transit passage,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in accordance with the provisions of the United Nations Convention on the Law of the Sea</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00023" title="Go to footnote 23" id="Backf00023">23</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person does not contravene the prohibition in regulation 45A(2) by conduct necessary to protect or avoid endangering—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the safety of any ship, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the life of any person.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Ships licences</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-25">25.</span>  After regulation 62 (trade licences), insert—&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegAmendingText">62B.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions in regulation 45A(1) and (2) (prohibition on chartering or operating specified ships) do not apply to anything done under the authority of a licence issued by the Secretary of State under this regulation.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Licences: general class="LegAmendingText">“, trade licences and ships licences”</span>.&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Ships: licensing offences</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-27">27.</span>  After regulation 66 (trade: licensing offences), insert—&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">Ships: licensing offences</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">66A.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (P”) commits an offence if P knowingly or recklessly—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">provides information that is false in a material respect, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">provides or produces a document that is not what it purports to be, for the purpose of obtaining a ships licence (whether for P or anyone else).</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who purports to act under the authority of a ships licence but who fails to comply with any condition of the licence commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A licence in respect of which an offence under paragraph (1) has been committed is to be treated as void from the time at which it was issued.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Penalties for offences</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-28">28.</span>  In regulation 79 (penalties for offences)—&#xD;</p><p insert <span class="LegAmendingText">“45A (prohibition on chartering or operating specified ships),”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-28-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in the opening words of paragraph (3), after “(trade: licensing offences),” insert <span class="LegAmendingText">“66A (ships: licensing offences),”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Jurisdiction to try offences</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-29">29.</span>  In regulation 81(1) (jurisdiction to try offences), in the opening words, after “(finance: licensing offences)” insert <span class="LegAmendingText">“, regulation 66A (ships: licensing offences)”</span>.&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Trade enforcement: application of CEMA</h3><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-30">30.</span>  In regulation 84(5) specified in chapter 90<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-31-p">(p)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">omit the entries for commodity codes 9031 80 20 and 9031 80 80.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearFix LegSignedSection"><div class="LegClearFix LegSignatory"><div class="LegClearFix LegSignee"><p class="LegPersonName">Stephen Doughty</p><p class="LegJobTitle">Minister of State</p><p class="LegDepartment">Foreign, Commonwealth and Development Office</p><p class="LegDateSigned">7th September 2026</p></div></div></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="Legislation-ExNote"></a><h2 class="LegExpNoteTitle">Explanatory Note</h2><p class="LegCommentText">(This note is not part of the Regulations)&#xD;</p> <p class="LegExpNoteText">These Regulations amend the Iran (Sanctions) (Nuclear) (EU Exit) Regulations 2019 (<a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2019/461">S.I. 2019/461</a>) (“<span class="LegTerm">the 2019 Regulations</span>”) and the Iran (Sanctions) Regulations 2023 (<a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2023/1314">S.I. 2023/1314</a>) (“<span class="LegTerm">the 2023 Regulations</span>”) using powers under the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 (c. 13)</a>.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">Regulations 3 to 13 amend the 2019 Regulations—&#xD;</p> <div><div class="LegRomanList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(i)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 4 amends regulations 2 and 3 to insert new definitions, and to ensure that prohibitions and requirements imposed in ships’ licences may be contravened by conduct outside the United Kingdom or in the territorial sea;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(ii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 5 amends regulation 5 of the 2019 Regulations to give the Secretary of State power to designate persons for the purposes of any of the new provisions inserted into the 2019 Regulations by these Regulations;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(iii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 6 imposes new financial measures including introducing prohibitions on: investments in relation to Iran; accounts or corresponding banking relationship with Iranian entities; the provision of insurance and reinsurance services to persons connected with Iran and on the sale or purchase of Iranian bonds;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(iv)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 7 imposes new trade sanctions in relation to gold, precious metals and diamonds; energy-related goods or technology; sectoral software goods and sectoral software and technology; specified relevant goods and technology; oil and petroleum products; petrochemicals and natural gas and ships. It also imposes sanctions on the provision and procurement of services relating to specified ships;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(v)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 8 imposes new measures in relation to Iranian aircraft and specified ships;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(vi)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 9 amends the provisions on exceptions and licences in Part 6, and regulations 10 and 11 amend the provisions on enforcement and maritime enforcement in Parts 8 and 9, respectively, of the 2019 Regulations;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(vii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 12 inserts a number of Schedules of restricted items into the 2019 Regulations.&#xD;</p></div></div></div>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">Regulations 14 to 31 amend the 2023 Regulations—&#xD;</p> <div><div class="LegRomanList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(i)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 15 defines “ships licence”. Regulation 16 amends regulation 3 to ensure that prohibitions and requirements imposed in ships’ licences may be contravened by conduct outside the United Kingdom or in the territorial sea;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(ii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 17 amends regulation 5 of the 2023 Regulations to give the Secretary of State power to designate persons for the purposes of new regulation 43B;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(iii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 18 imposes new sanctions on the provision of technical assistance, and the provision and procurement of services relating to specified ships;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(iv)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 19 imposes a prohibition on chartering or operating specified ships;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(v)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 20 prohibits the registration of specified ships;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(vi)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 21 amends regulation 51 on the specification of ships to reflect the new prohibitions relating to ships, and to extend the definition of “relevant activity”;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(vii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 22 amends the interpretation provisions for Part 7;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(viii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulations 23 to 26 amend the provisions on exceptions and licences in Part 8 of the 2023 Regulations;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(ix)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulations 27 to 30 amend the provisions on enforcement in Part 10 of the 2023 Regulations to reflect the new prohibitions;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(x)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">regulation 31 adds a number of items to, and removes some items from, Schedule 4 (Goods of strategic concern and technology of strategic concern).&#xD;</p></div></div></div>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">A full impact assessment has not been produced for this instrument as no, or no significant, impact on the private, voluntary or public sectors is foreseen. A de minimis assessment has been prepared as this instrument is likely to entail some costs for businesses, but the net impact is estimated to be below £10 million per year.&#xD;</p> <div class="LegFootnotes"><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnotesContainer"><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnote" id="f00001"><div class="LegFootnoteNoContainer">(<span class="LegFootnoteNo">1</span>)</div><p class="LegText">The power to make regulations under Part 1 of the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 (c. 13)</a> is conferred on an “appropriate Minister”. Section 1(9)(a) of the Act defines an “appropriate Minister” as including the Secretary of State.&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnote" id="f00002"><div class="LegFootnoteNoContainer">(<span class="LegFootnoteNo">2</span>)</div><p class="LegText"><a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 c. 13</a>. Sections 1, 11, 12 and 16 are amended by the Economic Crime (Transparency and Enforcement) Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2022/10">2022 (c. 10)</a>, Schedule 24, paragraph 443(1), and sections 57, 58, 59 and 65. Section 3A is inserted by the Economic Crime and Corporate Transparency Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2023/56">2023 (c. 56)</a>, section 35(3) and is in force only for the limited purpose of conferring power to make regulations. Section 17(5)(b)(i) (enforcement) is amended by the Sentencing Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2020/17">2020 (c. 17)</a>.&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnote" id="f00003"><div class="LegFootnoteNoContainer">(<span class="LegFootnoteNo">3</span>)</div><p class="LegText"><a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2019/461">S.I. 2019/461</a>, amended by <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2019/627">S.I. 2019/627</a>;
